## frames (audit / door offer / beats / finals / epilogue prompts)
--- d90 [the Table] phase=paths path=— ---
The ring of pale light. The too-many-angled faces. The trillions of eyes. The ring waits. Seven doors — some open, some priced beyond you. The choice is which risk you take. ◈ the Feared — 29 distinct monsters faced blow-by-blow. The footage exists, and it plays unasked. (standing: 100) ◇ the Indispensable — The Indispensable needs a full seat — the galaxy must actually need you, not just notice you. ◈ the Beloved — Viewership 35, fan favor 26 — the galaxy's favorite, by the numbers. (standing: 100) ◈ the Witness — 6 exposures on the record. The docket is real. (standing: 83) ◈ the Assimilated — Always open. The table takes anyone — that's what makes it a trap, and what makes it safe. (standing: 55) ◈ the Defiant — Defiance 4 on the record. The 'no' is earned. (standing: 46) ◈ the Relay — Stage-3 neural integration. The matrix doesn't care about your kingdom — it cares how deep you are. (sysbond 0) (standing: 50)

--- d90 [the Beloved] phase=challenge path=beloved ---
ACT TWO — THE VILLAGE PERFORMS. The whole Haven takes the stage: the feast song, the children's game, the thing they do at the fire when they think nobody's filming. Everybody's filming. Choose the centerpiece. ◈ YOUR BUILD — Act two has a slot nobody cast. The producers penciled you in weeks ago. audience 6
  > beat:song :: The feast song.
  > beat:game :: The children's game.
  > beat:risk :: The risky improv.
  > beat:build :: Do your own number. The produced one. (needs audience 5+)

--- d90 [the Beloved] phase=challenge path=beloved ---
ACT THREE — THE EDIT BAY. The producers slide the offer across the light: final cut on your own legend. Every bad moment — the deaths, the hunger, the things you said at the fire — gone. The audience will love the version of you that never existed. All it costs is the truth. audience 8
  > beat:cut :: Take the edit bay. (Edit Bay)
  > beat:raw :: Refuse the cut. Air it raw.

  > final:audience :: Let the audience speak.
  > final:contract :: Take the network contract.
